#### Bug fixes — 2026-09-24 (user-reported, batched)

- [x] **Exports did not look like the canvas** — done 2026-09-24, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D67. Reported against Word/PowerPoint paste: curved edges came out straight, the Nested Block came out blank, text fell back to serif, edge labels were clipped. **Root cause: `file/svgExport.ts` was a parallel reimplementation of the canvas that had drifted**, and every consumer routes through it (PNG, `.deepdraw.png`, SVG, PDF, print, PPTX, DOCX, copy-with-live-link, Slide Zoom thumbnails). Fixed by making the two share code rather than by patching the exporter: new `canvas/edgeRouting.ts` owns handle anchors and path building for both, `nesting/miniatureSvg.ts` owns the Nested Block thumbnail for both, and the exporter now resolves stencil defaults through `getStencilDefaultStyle` like the canvas does. Also fixed in passing: pictogram icons exported as outlined blobs, plain shapes exported at double line weight, and multi-line labels ran together on one line. Guarded by `file/exportParity.test.ts` and `tests/e2e/m13-export-parity.spec.ts`, which compares the real canvas path against the exported one. **No new dependencies.**
- [x] **The Shapes | Templates switcher looked pasted on** — done 2026-09-24, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D67. `.tabs` / `.tab` / `.tabActive` were referenced from `ShapeLibrary.tsx` but never written in `ShapeLibrary.module.css`, so a CSS module handed back `undefined` and the two rendered as bare browser buttons. Now a segmented control: recessed track, raised card for the selected tab, in the same gutter as the search box, working in both themes.

#### Quality — 2026-09-25 (chosen by the user from the post-Phase-5 options)

- [x] **Canvas/export render-parity sweep** — done 2026-09-25, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D72. New `tests/e2e/m14-render-parity.spec.ts` renders all 35 templates, and every diagram nested inside them, through both the canvas and the exporter, lines the elements up by model id, and compares what a reader sees: label text, line breaks, alignment, font, colour, fill and fill opacity, strokes, markers, dashes, vendor icons, the Nested Block's title, miniature and ports, and where every edge starts and ends. A kitchen-sink document covers what no template uses. **It found four real bugs on its first run:** (1) **the canvas had drawn no arrowheads since T2 (#40)** — React Flow was handed `url(#id)` where it wanted a bare id; (2) a port attachment was stored twice (`portId` and a `"port:<id>"` handle string) and kept in sync by nobody, so a template's port edge vanished, removing a port made its edge vanish from the canvas while it stayed in the file, and an edge dropped on a nested node ended at the side's midpoint instead of its own port dot; (3) corner handles exported 3.5px left of the canvas; (4) a node drawn before its stencil section loaded stayed blank for the whole session. All four fixed; each checked to be reported by the sweep when reintroduced. Also tightened the two T2/T3 E2E marker checks that had let (1) through by substring-matching a broken reference. New export option `annotateIds` (off by default — real exports are unchanged). **No new dependencies.**
- [x] **Test the exports in real Word and PowerPoint** — done 2026-09-25, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D74. User: "office is installed on this PC, start testing". Ten documents exported as `.docx` and both `.pptx` modes, opened in desktop Office via COM, plus a real "Copy image with live link" paste into both. **Five defects, all fixed:** (1) **PowerPoint refused every deck with speaker notes — i.e. every deck** — because the notes master shared the slide master's theme part; (2) every **Slide Zoom was misnamed** (`p166:zmPr`/`a:blipFill`/`a:spPr` instead of `pslz:zmPr`/`p166:blipFill`/`p166:spPr`), so zoom decks with nesting still failed after (1); (3) **Word could not see the embedded diagram**, related from the package root instead of the main document; (4) every Word export opened in **Compatibility Mode** (no `settings.xml`); (5) a pasted live-link image came into PowerPoint at **1.5× the slide's width**, because the 2x PNG declared no resolution. After the fixes all 20 decks and all 10 documents open, links resolve, notes and bookmarks are intact, Word sees the source, and the paste arrives at the diagram's real size with its link. Each fix has a test that fails on the old code. **Not verifiable here:** Word saving (PDF or re-save) — this Office install is not signed in and blocks every save until it is. **No new dependencies.**

The default label colour now contrasts with the fill behind it, in both themes and every export; it follows the theme only with no opaque fill behind it. Also fixed: the in-place editor froze every edited label to dark ink. Originally: — seen on screen while checking the import panel (2026-09-25). A shape's label colour follows the theme and turns light in dark mode, while outline shapes deliberately keep a white "paper" fill (`defaultStyle.ts`), so the text is light grey on white. Either the label should follow the shape's fill rather than the theme, or default fills should follow the theme too; the first keeps drawn diagrams looking the same in both themes, which is what draw.io does.

#### Redesign — added 2026-10-04 from `docs/DeepDraw_Redesign_Spec_20261004_v1.0.md`

Goal: DeepDraw stops looking like draw.io while behaving exactly as it does today — a change of surface, not of capability. Visual reference: `docs/DeepDraw_FullRedesign_Mockup_20261004_v1.0.html`, **except its change #5 (pages in the breadcrumb), which is dropped**: the page tab strip stays. Ship in this order, each on its own branch and PR, one PR open at a time, fully verified (unit, E2E, lint, typecheck, build), stopping for review after each. The spec's non-negotiable rules apply to every item: no default width/size changes, saved documents render identically, templates unchanged, no feature loss (an inventory table, old → new location, in the PR for R1 and R2), behaviour unchanged, existing tests updated to the new location rather than deleted, no accessibility regression.

Diagram settings move to a canvas chip beside the zoom control. A shortcut focuses the bar; Esc closes a popover, then clears the selection; the bar hides while dragging.

#### Diagram animation — 2026-10-07 (user brief; plan approved 2026-10-07)
Approved model: a typed, optional `motion` object — `DEdge.motion` (flow | packets | throughput, speed, volume), `NodeBase.motion` (pulse with optional ripple, health), `NestedNode.peek`, `Diagram.motion` (drawIn, one presentation mode with its order). Absent means exactly as today. **formatVersion 6, written only when a document uses motion** (`versionFor`), no-op 5→6 migration, FORMAT.md v6. Reduced motion stops everything; the share-link viewer gets its own Motion switch, the editor one in Preferences. Every option has a still resting state for exports. CSS animation (Packets via SVG `<animateMotion>`); only the open level is mounted and off-screen animation pauses. Authoring in the context bar (shape Motion popover; the line toolbar for edges); whole-diagram settings in the canvas chip's Page popover. Per-level count of continuously animated elements, no warning. No new dependencies, no default changes. One PR at a time, stop for review after each.

- [x] **A6 — Walkthrough, Build, Spotlight** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D123. **No new dependencies.** One mode per level, chosen in the Page popover; the order of steps is the shapes' reading order until the author moves one from a shape's or a line's Motion popover (lines can be steps). Walkthrough: numbered badges for everyone (its still form in every export) and a dot walking the path beneath the shapes. Build: steps appear in order in the share-link viewer, once a level; lines outside the order arrive with their later end. Spotlight: focus cycles in the viewer; a reader's trace takes over. In the editor, Build and Spotlight run only as a Preview (hint pill with Stop; Esc). Every motion switch shows the finished diagram. A deleted step stays in memory for undo and is dropped when the file is written.

#### Animated icons — 2026-10-07 (user brief; plan approved 2026-10-07; after A1, which builds the Motion switch)
18 original inline-SVG icons with CSS animation in a new **Animated** section under Icons, found by search beside their still twins. Ten twins exist (Settings, Sync, Database, Message Queue, Load Balancer, Lightning Bolt, Warning, Error, Schedule, Shield); the eight missing ones (running, scaling out, retrying, streaming, uploading, live traffic, healthy, heartbeat) are added as each icon's still resting pose (approved). Colours from `currentColor` and new tokens, resolved by the export path; a defined resting pose per icon rendered in every still export; render-parity coverage; checked at 24/32/48px; pan/zoom measured with 20 on screen.
- [x] **I1 — Compute and jobs** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D124. **No new dependencies.** Processing, Running, Scaling Out and Retrying in a new **Animated** section under Icons; still twins Run, Scale Out and Retry added to Misc (Processing's is Settings). Each body is its resting pose; CSS (`styles/animatedIcons.css`) moves parts through `nd-anim`, so every motion switch rests it; the library pauses them until hovered; miniatures and exports draw them still. Line work in `currentColor` = the shape's fill; accents from `--nd-icon-*` tokens, resolved by the exporter. Render parity covers them; checked at 24/32/48/64px and in dark mode; 20 on screen pan and zoom at 60 fps on the build.
- [x] **I2 — Data and storage** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D125. **No new dependencies.** Writes (twin: Database), Replicating (twin: Sync), Queue (twin: Message Queue) and Streaming (new twin: Stream, in Misc). Rests: half full, arrows still, three messages evenly spaced, waves standing.
- [x] **I3 — Traffic and network** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D126. **No new dependencies.** Uploading (new twin: Upload), Load Balancing (twin: Load Balancer), Live Traffic (new twin: Traffic) and Cache Hit (twin: Lightning Bolt). Rests: arrow up in place, a dot on each branch, every arc, the bolt lit.
- [x] **I4 — Status and operations** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D128. **No new dependencies.** Healthy (new twin: Healthy), Degraded (twin: Warning), Down (twin: Error) and Heartbeat (new twin: Heartbeat). Rests: the light on, the triangle fully shown, the disc centred, the heart at normal size.
- [x] **I5 — Time and trust** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D129. **No new dependencies.** Scheduled (twin: Schedule) and Protected (twin: Shield). Rests: the hand at 12, the shield at normal size. All 18 animated icons shipped.
- [x] **G1 — Animated GIF export** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D131. **No new dependencies** (encoder written in-repo). (user, 2026-10-08: "since we are giving motion in diagrams, allow the user to export the diagram as a GIF so it can be embedded in sites or social media posts"). In the Export menu beside PNG; frames rendered by the exporter at points in one motion cycle (every continuous kind, presentation modes, animated icons), so it shows what the canvas shows; a still diagram gives a one-frame GIF. Encoder written in-repo (no new dependency) unless that proves unworkable.

- [x] **G3 — A size option for the GIF** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D141. **No new dependencies.** (user, 2026-10-08: "add a size option to the GIF export".) Small, Medium or Large in a dialog, each showing this level's pixels; remembered.
- [x] **G4 — A frame rate option for the GIF** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D142. **No new dependencies.** (user, 2026-10-08: "add the frame rate option to the dialog".) Smooth (25 frames a second) or Lighter file (12.5), beside the size; remembered.
- [x] **G5 — Sharper GIFs: Extra large, and scaling up** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D143. **No new dependencies.** (user, 2026-10-08, with a screenshot of a Large GIF: "we should increase the size for clarity, more DPI may be required".) Extra large, 2400px; every size may scale a diagram up to twice its own size; the encoder streams frames so a large GIF fits in memory.

#### Nesting — proposed 2026-10-07 by the Q3 walk-through (needs user approval)
- [x] **N7 — The deletion prompts D12 deferred.** — done 2026-10-08, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D138. **No new dependencies.** Decided: "keep its contents here" (lift onto this level), not a pool. CLAUDE.md asks that deleting a Nested Block prompt "delete the child subtree, or detach it to an unused pool", and that deleting an inner node a port maps to prompt "remap or remove". D12 shipped silent cascades in v1 because the app had no dialogs; it has them now. Open question for the user: the "unused pool" has nowhere to be seen or reattached from — a pool needs a home (a "Detached levels" list in the app menu?) or the second choice becomes "keep its contents here" (lift the child's shapes onto this level). Undo already restores either delete.

#### Performance — proposed 2026-10-07 by Q4 (needs user approval; see `docs/performance-2026-10-07.md`)
- [x] **P1 — Level of detail when zoomed far out** — done 2026-10-07, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D115 (approved by the user, who asked for it to merge when green). **No new dependencies.** All 500 on screen: pan 15 → 82 fps. Below ~35% zoom, draw nodes as outline and fill only (no label text, handles, shadow; no arrowheads), back to full detail as the zoom rises. Targets the measured cost: with all 500 nodes on screen, panning runs at ~13 fps, mostly painting. Changes how a zoomed-out diagram looks; draw.io does not do it.

#### Inbox (ideas / discovered work — needs user approval to schedule)
- [x] **An error boundary around the app shell** — done 2026-10-01, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D81. Each region of the shell has its own boundary, so a failure shows a small notice with Try again while the rest of the app, autosave included, keeps working; a last-resort boundary around the whole app offers "Save a copy of the diagram".
- [x] **Edit ▸ Paste and the canvas menu's Paste read the system clipboard** — done 2026-10-01, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D82. An image copied in another app is placed (at the click point from the canvas menu); otherwise, or if the browser refuses access, the shapes copied here are pasted as before.
- [x] **Map draw.io's embedded images on import** — done 2026-10-01, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D82. Embedded pictures become image nodes, each stored once; linked ones are never fetched and stay rectangles with a notice that selects them.
- [x] **Finish the Office check once Office is signed in** — done 2026-09-28, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D76. In the user's own running Word and PowerPoint: Word saved all 10 `.docx` exports as PDF; three exports edited and saved in Word reopened in DeepDraw with a byte-identical diagram (Word kept our part as `item1.xml`, CDATA intact); and clicking a Slide Zoom in a real slideshow of a post-D75 deck opened its slide. Also confirmed D74's Word fix was necessary: a package built the pre-D74 way lost its diagram source on its first save in Word.
- [x] **Slide Zoom fallback for PowerPoint builds without zoom** — done 2026-09-28, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D75. Each zoom is now written in the exact `mc:AlternateContent` envelope PowerPoint uses, with the cover as a hyperlinked picture in the fallback. Also fixed: the zoom image's inner offset, which we wrote as the slide position instead of 0,0.
- [x] **T1 and T2 templates never name their edge handles** — done 2026-09-25, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D73. The flowchart and mind-map starters name their sides by hand; the eight T2 templates use a new `faceEdges()` that fills in only unnamed ends from the layout. The catalogue test now requires every edge in every template to name both ends unless an end is a port.
- [x] **Keywords for the `general` and `basic` stencil sections** — done 2026-09-25, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D73. 36 shapes. The Nested Block is now found by "drill down", "subprocess" and "zoom in". A guard requires keywords on every shape in each fully-covered section and names the three that are not yet.

Its first run on Word found three real schema violations in `word/styles.xml` (heading `spacing` after `outlineLvl`), now fixed.
- [x] **More worked examples under `examples/`** — done 2026-09-25, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D73. `incident-runbook` and `online-store-domain`, chosen to exercise what nothing else does. Every example is now provably reproducible — the Azure one was not, despite its README.
- [x] **A better surface for import warnings than `window.alert`** — done 2026-09-25, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D73. `ImportNoticesPanel`: stays until closed, and each note's Show opens its page and selects exactly the affected shapes.
- ~~Custom / importable shape libraries + net-new stencil sections~~ — descoped 2026-09-28 by the user (D77): with no server, a personal library could only ever live in one browser's storage, so it would not follow the user or the file. Replaced by **Insert your own image into a diagram** (queued under "Next" above), which travels inside the `.deepdraw` file.
- [x] **SVG export needs the typeface embedded** — done 2026-09-24 as part of the export-parity fix, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D67. Resolved by embedding, not by subsetting or by converting to paths: `file/exportFonts.ts` base64s the two latin woff2 weights the app already ships (400 and 700 — the only two `RichLabel.bold` can select) into an `@font-face` block inside the exported SVG, loaded on demand so it stays out of the main bundle. ~60 KB per exported SVG, text stays selectable. **The reasoning that deferred this was wrong**: leaving the system stack did not avoid the fallback problem, it guaranteed it — and edge labels named no family at all, so they came out serif. PDF is fixed by the same change, since it rasterises this SVG.
- ~~"Selection bleed" (clicking one shape appeared to also select an unrelated group's icons)~~ — resolved as NOT a bug, see CLAUDE_HISTORY D34. Frame-by-frame analysis of the user's own screen recording showed every "problem" instance was a genuine click-and-drag producing a real, correctly-sized rubber-band selection box that swept over multiple shapes — standard draw.io/Figma-style behavior, not app misbehavior.
- ~~Right-click context menu doesn't open for a node that's part of an active multi-selection~~ — fixed 2026-08-25, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D46. Root cause was React Flow's own `nodesselection` overlay swallowing the right-click; it has a dedicated `onSelectionContextMenu` hook for exactly this.
- ~~Page size setting (fixed-size pages for print/PDF layout)~~ — done 2026-08-25 alongside PDF export/print, see CLAUDE_HISTORY.md D46.
